# DOCX Import Review: 7.1 争锋竞技场简易百科.docx

## Imported Body

<!-- article-paragraph:non-prose -->
争锋竞技场简易百科
<!-- source-body:1:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:9b0fc7d2a7af50f9:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:non-prose -->
前言
<!-- source-body:2:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:0c03907eda8e9882:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
争锋竞技场（以下简称争锋）作为拂晓的三大核心玩法之一，也是唯一的PVP部分，入门即享保底津贴，高于普通竞技场最高档，可以说是非常休闲了。但若你想让自己的舰灵发光发热，一展风采，那么争锋将是你最好的选择。
<!-- source-body:3:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:9ef7e638a48c7beb:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
与人斗其乐无穷，争锋有蓝、紫、金三个场次，所需舰灵数量多，配队较复杂，装备要求高。为方便萌新快速入门、老登快速上手，本攻略将从PVP基础知识、配装思路和参考配队三个方面入手，让读者正确的认识、体验争锋的快乐。
<!-- source-body:4:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:211fd398fa6ef39a:paragraph -->

# 一、综合篇
<!-- source-body:5:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:e5e2bdef6652a5f8:paragraph -->

## （一）入门
<!-- source-body:6:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:ea6625a4b5704943:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
如果你是新玩家，又想快速上手，那么准备38个+10对海雷达（即俗称的马桶，含特殊马桶-勋章、蛋糕），所有出战角色装备+6启用尽量提高，技能拉高，队伍旗舰9级或10级拉满，前述条件完成就算入门了。接下来慢慢养成补强其余内容即可（包括好感、羁绊等）。但如果想精进一步，那么请耐心阅读本百科。
<!-- source-body:7:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:32e67fa825be71f1:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
牢记，随机数大于一切，即便完全镜像队伍也会出现各种随机变化，有时甚至可以逆转克制关系。因此不必因为几次的失利而焦虑。
<!-- source-body:8:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a1de68b4446faaef:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
PS1：想要更完整、详细的信息请去wiki自行查阅，为方便理解本文只介绍简化的核心要素。
<!-- source-body:9:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:b5b76f97f6b3e8c9:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
PS2：竞技场（普通、争锋）的舰灵全部为出战位，支援技不生效。
<!-- source-body:10:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:d85f1a8605a01910:paragraph -->

## （二）参数/公式相关
<!-- source-body:11:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:269ede9f2052af06:paragraph -->

### 1.索敌/制空判定
<!-- source-body:12:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:46b8b624969e1531:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
和pve同样的计算规则
<!-- source-body:13:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:c5680d20ad920616:paragraph -->

#### ①索敌判定
<!-- source-body:14:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:6dc9cd6d36ec7eaa:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
我方舰队索敌大于敌方时获得“索敌成功”buff（我方命中率+10%，敌方命中率-10%），同时判定为同航战（敌我双方炮击|雷击伤害+8%，命中值+10%）；
<!-- source-body:15:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:6eb2860e6a8dbbdb:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
当我方索敌大于敌方索敌的1.25倍时，判定为T优势（我方雷击|炮击伤害+15%，命中值+20%；敌方雷击|炮击伤害-15%，命中值-10%）。
<!-- source-body:16:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:93e082609768fa92:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
我方舰队索敌小于敌方时，不获得任何buff，同时判定为反航战（敌我双方炮击|雷击伤害-8%，命中值-10%）。
<!-- source-body:17:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:965c7ad8d1fae2cb:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
当我方索敌小于敌方索敌0.8倍时，判定为T劣势（我方雷击|炮击伤害-15%，命中值-10%；敌方雷击|炮击伤害+15%，命中值+20%）。
<!-- source-body:18:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:7b30cbd5c7ba543e:paragraph -->

#### ②制空判定
<!-- source-body:19:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:da8b89f1c53e16ae:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
类似索敌，但是判定条件为航空值的10%，30%。
<!-- source-body:20:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a7aad99520c3948c:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:21:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a49364e878c259a9:paragraph -->

#### 小结：
<!-- source-body:22:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:ec2ac5e6f914bc62:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
进攻防守索敌线不同，进攻高于对方0.75倍索敌不会T劣，防守需高于对方0.8倍索敌。
<!-- source-body:23:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:22142096162b26ec:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
绝大多数配队要避免陷入T劣状态，相对而言航队受影响会低一些。
<!-- source-body:24:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:fbb6c0d2511d0222:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
通常来说，同航反航区别不是很大，但是吃闪避的阵容会被航向影响相关数值。因此攻防配队可以主动调整索敌值，以应对不同的属性需求。
<!-- source-body:25:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:1786aae73d1b588b:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
制空的影响相对较小，主要看航母数量和影响航空输出。
<!-- source-body:26:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:35ddc5efdbeefa8d:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
老登常说的“科技”：在满足反航索敌的需求下，将部分对海雷达进行其他设备替换（例如炮弹、滑板、飞机），满足额外的生存|输出需求。
<!-- source-body:27:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:dfb606cc3d0e3af7:paragraph -->

## 2.增伤和减伤
<!-- source-body:28:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:91af34ff0ae68cd2:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
进入争锋PVP所有舰灵都务必誓约，最低要求八槽。最优是永恒好感度，提供20%增伤和20%减伤。
<!-- source-body:29:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:0225989ff5c4c106:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
游戏内加减伤均为加算，请尽可能优先堆减伤，来源天赋、旗舰、装备、技能等。
<!-- source-body:30:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:4c98cc2fa7d5f963:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:31:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:e5e401c7a2121415:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
以白板永恒誓约船为例：
<!-- source-body:32:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:4c36daf9cbbbaa1e:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
A.选择减伤25%天赋，实际坦度增幅为1-0.55/0.8=0.3125
<!-- source-body:33:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:43666dd94918104d:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
B.选择增伤25%天赋，实际伤害增幅为1.45/1.2-1=0.2083
<!-- source-body:34:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:ea013587e761ad97:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
当数值提高时，此差距会被进一步放大：
<!-- source-body:35:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:0c2be31b4a12f0c4:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
A.额外减伤50%，实际坦度增幅为1-0.3/0.8=0.625
<!-- source-body:36:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:119f19fcf51c5fbc:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
B.额外增伤50%，实际伤害增幅为1.7/1.2-1=0.4167
<!-- source-body:37:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:e04f528bd846098d:paragraph -->

## 3.局内外加成
<!-- source-body:38:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:b0463b4e327d3ee6:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
实际面板=（编队界面面板+对局中固定加成）*对局内增益百分比
<!-- source-body:39:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:6768964e21ca74d6:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
对局内增益包括：航向、旗舰、阵型、技能和赛季buff。
<!-- source-body:40:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:20d214b7e2ec9228:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
例如伊丽莎白旗舰，复纵阵，面板200机动，实际为（200+90）*1.25=362.5
<!-- source-body:41:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:064600fd06dd396d:paragraph -->

## 4.弹种克制
<!-- source-body:42:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:0a4a9f97a0fa4a27:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
同PVE，游戏内可查看详细的数值和克制关系。也可查询弹种伤害修正表。
<!-- source-body:43:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f8e235d960fa66d9:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
特别备注，燃烧属于纯粹伤害，按照面板数值直接扣除。
<!-- source-body:44:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:6d4231fe83316490:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:45:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:cd0762f701e98c7a:paragraph -->

## 5.幸运值
<!-- source-body:46:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:06d5961f5c032d7d:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
影响基础暴击率和被暴击率，装备来源主要是飞机和蛋糕。
<!-- source-body:47:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:cc0984d9ef847ac4:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
基础暴击率={1-[守方幸运/(守方幸运+攻方幸运/3)]^0.8}×80%（最高70%）
<!-- source-body:48:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:24342f68c4a284c9:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
最终暴击率=基础暴击率+加成暴击率-守方防暴击率（来自技能、阵型、装备等）
<!-- source-body:49:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:dbc7d855d469195a:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
相同幸运值基础暴击率为16.44%。
<!-- source-body:50:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:aad56af37e4f038d:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
因此优先需要考虑核心输出舰灵的幸运，此外幸运太低的舰灵通常不适合做主T。
<!-- source-body:51:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:59c397c83d4910cc:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:52:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:b4971b97c5fe7e4f:paragraph -->

## 6.命中与机动
<!-- source-body:53:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:d23bd00fdc1e774e:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
装备搭配是争锋博弈的首要考量要素。
<!-- source-body:54:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:e8f88e49009f5fa3:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
基础命中率=攻方命中/（攻方命中+守方机动）×100%
<!-- source-body:55:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f811367342ac40d6:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
最终命中率=基础命中率+加成命中率-守方回避率
<!-- source-body:56:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:4de65862ea1420cb:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
【阈值：最高命中95%，最低命中5%】
<!-- source-body:57:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:c2af9c8c4995a0e9:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
由于舰灵都有基础机动，基础闪避不为0，考虑到血量天赋的固定加成和实际机动情况后，往往是同级别闪避天赋&gt;血量天赋。携带机动装置或有闪避技能、天赋等情况会进一步放大差距。例如10%的闪避和10%血量天赋，基础闪避10%的情况下，实际收益为：1-0.8/0.9=0.1111＞0.1。
<!-- source-body:58:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:7b41c610f0dfe11c:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
在拂晓中，通过装备提升生存的最有效手段就是携带+10金色机动装置。这是在满足索敌需求的情况下，生存位最推荐携带的装备。
<!-- source-body:59:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:d2b8c97368c0f28f:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:60:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:741eb802a5ed3c30:paragraph -->

## 7.伤害计算公式
<!-- source-body:61:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:a151e81069c74a5c:paragraph -->

### ①对空伤害
<!-- source-body:62:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:815205b458069cdf:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
通常不考虑，因为PVP实战不明显，很难打下飞机。
<!-- source-body:63:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:6fa5e0c90d7725a2:paragraph -->

### ②注意乘区
<!-- source-body:64:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:8fe2370d965d7553:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
XX伤害提高和XX值提高是独立乘区，例如：炮击伤害提高50%和炮击+50%，实际是1.5*1.5=2.25的伤害提升（不考虑别的buff）。类似的还有航空和雷击。
<!-- source-body:65:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:d7f9da5199e8c225:paragraph -->

### ③攻击力的计算
<!-- source-body:66:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:80470bf6348b7861:paragraph -->

#### （1）炮击/雷击攻击力：
<!-- source-body:67:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:80ed519d24c3b57d:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
炮击|雷击*炮击|雷击伤害百分比
<!-- source-body:68:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a708ae6bab67f39b:paragraph -->

#### （2）航空攻击力：
<!-- source-body:69:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:90cc92809d8600af:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（航空值+轰炸|鱼雷）*航空伤害百分比
<!-- source-body:70:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:1396d10a20a2ebb1:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
技能有额外加成的攻击力，根据描述的最终伤害类型归类。如（炮击+对空）的炮击伤害即为炮击伤害。
<!-- source-body:71:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:674b609699502d52:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
此外攻击力增加也是独立乘区。
<!-- source-body:72:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:2f331228d0dc5103:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
因此能吃到不同乘区的舰灵往往是强者
<!-- source-body:73:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:c670cb58985869fe:paragraph -->

### ④伤害计算公式（简化）
<!-- source-body:74:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:ae778c0e91ba72bd:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
受到伤害=(攻击力/(攻击力+1.3*防御力))*弹种补正
<!-- source-body:75:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:44b9c0c9517e30e9:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
防御力对应装甲和对空。
<!-- source-body:76:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:beec4b39ee30f2d0:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
特别的，高平炮和撞击近战对应装甲。
<!-- source-body:77:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:4c2d4bb45e2a9120:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
防御力通常比攻击力低很多，因此大多数情况下增加防御的收益没有很高。
<!-- source-body:78:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:11a6256f36d6ba06:paragraph -->

## 8.装填
<!-- source-body:79:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:2bf2694ca8715cb9:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
一般作用于战列战巡，详见主副炮装填表。通常不用考虑，特别是轻母航母装填收益很低。
<!-- source-body:80:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:b31438c2fa9f4edb:paragraph -->

## （三）站位
<!-- source-body:81:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:13d8398a0af26d4d:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
队伍里舰灵站位通常遵循以下规则：1号，3号负责承受伤害，其中1号是主T可以不追求伤害，3号是副T也会承受较多伤害；2号在1号倒下后会变成T；4号、5号通常是要保护的核心输出|旗舰；6号可作保护位但本身会吃一些伤害（被锁可能直接去世）。
<!-- source-body:82:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f2302776e06ebd3f:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
不同舰灵由于攻击距离范围等因素会使站位规则出现偏差，如炮系排头遇见航系排头可能会罚站。航系开头的2、3、6号可能更容易吃伤害。
<!-- source-body:83:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:19bdb17c136e4b5d:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
请根据个人实战体验来调整。也可直接借鉴参考阵容
<!-- source-body:84:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:1f9e89d1b6a47558:paragraph -->

## （四）舰灵天赋
<!-- source-body:85:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:5248421ad43e769a:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
查询舰灵PVP天赋图表即可，可根据敌方索敌等实际使用需求微调。
<!-- source-body:86:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:01b28f9d537f380d:paragraph -->

## （五）舰灵装备
<!-- source-body:87:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:762419719d6ea2cf:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
有需要可以查询通用装备速查表和wiki图鉴了解属性，名称和效果，以及对应获取渠道，培养优先级等。
<!-- source-body:88:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:7f1dc6b45c4097ba:paragraph -->

## （六）信物
<!-- source-body:89:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:212f035afa2eed8b:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
仅从PVP争锋角度，目前版本推荐优先级如下：
<!-- source-body:90:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:4eee3b502745df68:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
隼鹰=宁海&gt;南达科他=平海&gt;应瑞&gt;标枪&gt;唐斯&gt;舍尔
<!-- source-body:91:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:eb0afe907bf2af2c:paragraph -->

## （七）阵型
<!-- source-body:92:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:a7ace0661c0c6a85:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
游戏内可查看，具体效果同PVE，实战可根据参考阵容。
<!-- source-body:93:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a080a6efd30f2434:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
简记：单纵炮雷；复纵机动；轮型航空；T字暴击很少用。
<!-- source-body:94:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:9d4d78af722b28c7:paragraph -->

## （八）赛季天气
<!-- source-body:95:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:c0c455fb6303659c:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
S35赛季开始新引入的系统，游戏内可查看场地buff。
<!-- source-body:96:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:55fb5999fbac5a40:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:97:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:e33c10b3af791425:paragraph -->

## （九）通用法则
<!-- source-body:98:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:8dd58e801a37e334:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
①输出位装备高命中高输出，生存位高机动高功能性。
<!-- source-body:99:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:49c3545b25906d02:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
②偏机动队尽量进攻反航，防守同航。
<!-- source-body:100:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:30e2e308359324d9:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
③T优太难，全索敌往往只有同航，收益未必有关键位置科技的反航高。
<!-- source-body:101:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:8c8b7fef965019a6:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
④灵活根据自己分段附近主流队伍变阵，通常需要满足索敌条件，攻防装备和阵容均可变
<!-- source-body:102:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:3432522a9622406c:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:103:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:ba519d19ce2d3e91:paragraph -->

# 二、争锋竞技场简介
<!-- source-body:104:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:ee83bec22dfad97c:paragraph -->

## （一）争锋机制介绍
<!-- source-body:105:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:7d4ef8fef3fb7e4a:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
争锋分为三个场次，海岸警备、主力舰队、无畏舰队，也就是我们常说的蓝场、紫场和金场。蓝场只能上蓝绿白，紫场可以上紫蓝绿白，金场无限制。但是改造前后的舰灵视为同名角色，不能出现在同一场次。
<!-- source-body:106:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:b2542b36f92aae3c:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
每天总计五次，可自由选择分配对战场次。每次对战需要打小中大三小场，三场分数独立计算。每日奖励以三场分数之和的最高排位百分比于21：00结算。
<!-- source-body:107:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:725fa5f38d22d10f:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
不同于普通竞技场防守阵容的自由设置，争锋的防守阵容取决于自己最后一次进攻的阵容记录。意味着你打完后，升级、改造、强化等、改变装备、学习技能等都要到下次进攻才会刷新纪录。
<!-- source-body:108:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:0a96c170e78b0446:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:109:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:8535054eb9e5b5a4:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
分差补偿机制：分数相同或相近时每小场胜利+12分，失败-10分。战胜比你高出一定分数的玩家，得分额外增加，分差越大加的越多，如+14+16…，失败扣分减少，如-9-8…。反之输给比你少于一定分数的玩家，得分增加减少，如+11+10…，若失败扣分固定为-10，进攻超时扣分固定-6。
<!-- source-body:110:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:d4efccaa46e0b6b3:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:111:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a6fa2815691f9105:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
每赛季每场对局的第一次强制选择80级人机对手。由于分差机制，如果你想起步高或者刷最高排名，建议一开服就去打，去迟了打赢人机只有+7+6。
<!-- source-body:112:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:0f4604848e0fe2b0:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:113:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:81d3da293f2996a3:paragraph -->

## （二）争锋编队构成与配装简介
<!-- source-body:114:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:9d6badafa3376bb4:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
属性对比皆为同场内比较，取平均水平。由于装备体系不同，相比炮系，航系实际机动要高一些，实际命中要低一些，因此加入飞机装备后考量。
<!-- source-body:115:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:ee3758a53717d781:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
未特别提及的场合，设备均选用对海雷达（或蛋糕、勋章）；生存位选机动装置，带有鱼雷防御（鱼雷|鱼雷机伤害减免10%）天赋的可选普列赛。
<!-- source-body:116:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:0873392444ca4cba:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:117:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:146f6cbe60045d50:paragraph -->

### 1.小船场
<!-- source-body:118:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:b03ab4233531e7b0:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
由驱逐，补给（女灶神），水母（镇海）组成。
<!-- source-body:119:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:388196033574e2d2:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
由于女灶神四个设备位，镇海提供制空和高索敌，这两个可以说必带。
<!-- source-body:120:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:16b5cd5f5455c070:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:82c25b46586a099d" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:82c25b46586a099d:4:4">低</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:82c25b46586a099d:4:5">高</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:82c25b46586a099d:4:6">低</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:121:src-7d080e8d651b:table:82c25b46586a099d:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:non-prose -->
通用装备推荐：
<!-- source-body:122:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:788dd08cc7fd79e2:paragraph -->

#### ①驱逐
<!-- source-body:123:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:30ca514ae2a00694:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
主炮：根据命中选择305和e1，输出优先305；
<!-- source-body:124:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:54e8528bd3671290:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
鱼雷：金彩均可；
<!-- source-body:125:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:6301df59ef691757:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
防空炮:推荐紫喷进。
<!-- source-body:126:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:e229aac36f1c6327:paragraph -->

#### ②补给（女灶神）
<!-- source-body:127:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:61e10a70979f08c0:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
如图所示，反航场合带1-3个机动装置。
<!-- source-body:128:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:70265475ce709a41:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:129:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:997a7086bf43888c:paragraph -->

#### ③水母（镇海）
<!-- source-body:130:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:52a6035e095b5cf5:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
如图所示，分别为同航和反航。反航也可双火控。
<!-- source-body:131:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f2f3a10f616ac09b:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:132:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:b8b2d51d393e3969:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
如图所示，分别为同航和反航。反航也可双火控。
<!-- source-body:133:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f8821a4a22dcdd6e:paragraph -->

### 2.中船场
<!-- source-body:134:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:9bffe81e17fe58e7:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
由轻母、轻巡、重巡，重炮和雷巡组成。
<!-- source-body:135:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:330c6ffa92453692:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:4c7e433ddeeff23c" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:4c7e433ddeeff23c:4:6">高</td></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:4c7e433ddeeff23c:5:1">重炮</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:4c7e433ddeeff23c:5:2">低</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:4c7e433ddeeff23c:5:3">低</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:4c7e433ddeeff23c:6:2">低</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:4c7e433ddeeff23c:6:3">中</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:4c7e433ddeeff23c:6:4">高</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:4c7e433ddeeff23c:6:5">高</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:4c7e433ddeeff23c:6:6">低</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:136:src-7d080e8d651b:table:4c7e433ddeeff23c:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:non-prose -->
通用装备推荐：
<!-- source-body:137:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:7b27f1c498eb1552:paragraph -->

#### ①轻母
<!-- source-body:138:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:0c56ded091e0c0b1:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（1）战斗机：通用烈风和海毒牙几乎没区别，烈风略好，本身轻母就是高对空，一点数值可忽略，多点机动万一呢。输出可选命中战斗机；
<!-- source-body:139:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:7e0b1b03c3895c65:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（2）轰炸机：通用萤火虫，需求索敌则选索敌SBD轰炸机；
<!-- source-body:140:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:03db00115326db3d:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（3）鱼雷机：输出向全部换金剑鱼，除了鱼雷数值，幸运价值非常高，防暴击也加暴击，流星拉胯不考虑，需求索敌就选择紫剑鱼；
<!-- source-body:141:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:4fda02b8b221671a:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（4）防空炮（部分轻母可带）：性价比角度用紫喷进，补命中可用金防空。
<!-- source-body:142:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:20e8685defa34294:paragraph -->

#### ②轻巡
<!-- source-body:143:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:62e05704ae1ee2e3:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（1）主炮：通用金三联装，或按需补命中的稀有UR和金；
<!-- source-body:144:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:6bbcfee428fd4719:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（2）副炮：e1，补炮击对空；
<!-- source-body:145:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:6a08afa1f4a0eb83:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（3）鱼雷：金雷均可，区别不大；
<!-- source-body:146:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:c65b5132da22e02f:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（4）防空炮：紫喷，输出位可换金。
<!-- source-body:147:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:982f97c78d0056b7:paragraph -->

#### ③重巡
<!-- source-body:148:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:1dff6c1e53d389aa:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（1）主炮：轻母多时对空炮最佳，三联mk15，205也可；
<!-- source-body:149:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:cd871cdabe91896d:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（2）副炮：e1即可；
<!-- source-body:150:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a03634b4958fb714:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（3）鱼雷：推荐使用磁雷，补命中；
<!-- source-body:151:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:30f76ff6bc5e3a35:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（4）防空炮：抗伤选紫喷，输出金防空。
<!-- source-body:152:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:c7e4305a261eb8c4:paragraph -->

#### ④重炮（刚需高命中）
<!-- source-body:153:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:3d91284c2b256fe9:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（1）主炮：高命中战列炮，如406UR，双联装361，或通用的mk406；
<!-- source-body:154:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:2cef6e50e9b07a44:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（2）副炮：双联mk5驱逐炮或者e1；
<!-- source-body:155:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:395f4704b81e1a76:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（3）防空炮：金防空；
<!-- source-body:156:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:cb55d7fafbae3d3e:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（4）设备：推荐彩火控|274火控雷达。
<!-- source-body:157:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a754ca60110da40a:paragraph -->

#### ⑤雷巡
<!-- source-body:158:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:94b8526bc69f3587:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（1）主炮：需要命中和装填，用蓝色三联skc150，或者带命中装填的金彩轻巡炮；
<!-- source-body:159:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:66d6997a2adae11e:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（2）鱼雷：金磁雷补命中；
<!-- source-body:160:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:2d71a697dc6fe8f4:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（3）设备：可以给蛋糕，补装填和幸运。
<!-- source-body:161:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:d9b2efa5a33f2aba:paragraph -->

### 3.大船场
<!-- source-body:162:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:9e00f073f4edf682:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
由战列、战巡、航母，装母和航战（新）组成。
<!-- source-body:163:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:9fd9303e44a6148f:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:f5e2d30d77696e7a" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" <!-- source-body:164:src-7d080e8d651b:table:f5e2d30d77696e7a:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:non-prose -->
通用装备推荐：
<!-- source-body:165:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f5195b0c9890afbb:paragraph -->

#### ①战列，战巡（CD长单发炮弹，需要高命中）
<!-- source-body:166:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:6ecf59be22081552:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
由于部分战列战巡有装填需求，可根据具体情况使用特定装备来调整。
<!-- source-body:167:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:dc0a8922a27094be:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（1）主炮：通用mk6，彩炮更好；
<!-- source-body:168:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:e132987ed5c4dd3e:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（2）副炮：推荐双联mk5驱逐炮等高命中炮，蓝场使用轻巡三联炮；
<!-- source-body:169:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:fd76f08e622ed349:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（3）防空炮：金防空，彩防空。
<!-- source-body:170:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:79f9650a06ffa6ba:paragraph -->

#### ②装母，航母
<!-- source-body:171:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:87ef6998ada895d9:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（1）战斗机：同轻母，烈风即可，输出可带命中飞机，UR最好；
<!-- source-body:172:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f52efa5f569fddf2:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（2）轰炸机：同轻母，萤火虫即可，输出位可用sbd索敌|幸运；
<!-- source-body:173:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f7bf1836701fc3ff:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（3）鱼雷机：天河拉索敌和生存，输出位可选金剑鱼。
<!-- source-body:174:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:5502e7a46e8e0af9:paragraph -->

#### ③航战
<!-- source-body:175:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:8cb11f3a56aa4a01:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（1）主炮：通用mk6即可；
<!-- source-body:176:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:6df919a53222ade0:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（2）防空炮：金防空。航战缺命中不推荐紫喷进，真要用也得需配合天赋、机动阵等；
<!-- source-body:177:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:443f50a184e59932:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（3）水侦：金瑞云，高索敌。
<!-- source-body:178:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f97e5acfe213724a:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:179:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a2940518dfe714d7:paragraph -->

# 三、参考阵容推荐
<!-- source-body:180:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:a40bd0e9bc37c133:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
本攻略会给出一些常见主流阵容，并简单介绍优缺点。有特别配装的情况单独提及，其余用默认配装和法则调整即可。
<!-- source-body:181:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:3f3e8d033ca0cb89:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
关于天气系统的影响：属于天意的大手。一般来说主流队伍都较为通用，个别极端情况请自己分析调整。
<!-- source-body:182:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:7c906a614631707a:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
推荐先练通用船，练船避免“追”阵容。在当前争锋有天气影响的情况下，刚练好某些船下赛季就不好用的情况会比较常见，所以不要盲目照抄。
<!-- source-body:183:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a9de719e761463ba:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:184:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:9d0e24cd1a601998:paragraph -->

## （一）金场
<!-- source-body:185:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:6d17bf701a6f1f9b:paragraph -->

### 1.金小船
<!-- source-body:186:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:e85406d029d2c7c3:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
由于金水侦的加入，目前版本索敌线比之前略高，推荐防守索敌在1353以上。
<!-- source-body:187:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:c075530aabc5b433:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
根据弹种装甲克制关系，优先选择高爆弹。
<!-- source-body:188:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:d9fa93eba843f19b:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
默认阵型推荐复纵|单纵，实战个人偏好复纵。
<!-- source-body:189:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a93d1a4e5e350064:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:190:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:5fd2a2a2f73c1ea1:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
参考阵容
<!-- source-body:191:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:ef52627ae556c93f:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
当前常态优选阵容比较固定，默认为：女灶神，丹阳，小拉菲改，卡米，镇海，夕立改。
<!-- source-body:192:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:4d59f18dd0ab90b8:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
下位备选：杰维斯，火力，绫波改，z1改，岛风
<!-- source-body:193:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:0739b788730ec721:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
旗舰只有丹阳和女灶神。
<!-- source-body:194:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:d8f1d7bc5d9ba778:paragraph -->

#### ①优选同航阵容（全马桶）
<!-- source-body:195:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:701b344407cf35c7:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7077eeddc52dfeda" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7077eeddc52dfeda" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7077eeddc52dfeda:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:43737cff48ae333e --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7077eeddc52dfeda:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d5bc34b48d5405c9 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7077eeddc52dfeda:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d10a13a942a1b8c4 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7077eeddc52dfeda:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:b3b8201d26980a8a --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7077eeddc52dfeda:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:c76f5894748fcf57 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7077eeddc52dfeda:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:4b2a9e9de852cc92 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7077eeddc52dfeda:2:1">女灶神</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7077eeddc52dfeda:2:2">丹阳</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7077eeddc52dfeda:2:3">小拉菲改</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7077eeddc52dfeda:2:4">卡米契亚</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7077eeddc52dfeda:2:5">镇海</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7077eeddc52dfeda:2:6">夕立改</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:196:src-7d080e8d651b:table:7077eeddc52dfeda:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
②优选反航阵容
<!-- source-body:197:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:08f6c5532fd41120:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166e0a3ef6015d60" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166e0a3ef6015d60" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166e0a3ef6015d60:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:434de921ed7f7dd6 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166e0a3ef6015d60:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:9c847e57a8805789 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166e0a3ef6015d60:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:09d45e1aac46ce4b --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166e0a3ef6015d60:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:9cbe3bf67ab5f3a0 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166e0a3ef6015d60:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:0594556a50081fbf --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166e0a3ef6015d60:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:6ebfeee7b7e2800f --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166e0a3ef6015d60:2:1">女灶神</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166e0a3ef6015d60:2:2">丹阳</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166e0a3ef6015d60:2:3">小拉菲改</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166e0a3ef6015d60:2:4">卡米契亚</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166e0a3ef6015d60:2:5">镇海</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166e0a3ef6015d60:2:6">夕立改</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:198:src-7d080e8d651b:table:166e0a3ef6015d60:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:non-prose -->
备选阵容示例：
<!-- source-body:199:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:3db30b505de2d070:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:df744e0ffd348045" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:df744e0ffd348045" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:df744e0ffd348045:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:0de9ca3514169259 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:df744e0ffd348045:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:36c8c912ee0ca842 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:df744e0ffd348045:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:fadeefec9478e375 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:df744e0ffd348045:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:45093408335bf85b --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:df744e0ffd348045:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:935f324d359a283e --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:df744e0ffd348045:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:06721dc8743da87b --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:df744e0ffd348045:2:1">女灶神</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:df744e0ffd348045:2:2">丹阳</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:df744e0ffd348045:2:3">岛风</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:df744e0ffd348045:2:4">卡米契亚</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:df744e0ffd348045:2:5">夕立改</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:df744e0ffd348045:2:6">镇海</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:200:src-7d080e8d651b:table:df744e0ffd348045:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑是极地天气小拉菲无法使用的特供版，后续同上
<!-- source-body:201:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:dee8049c1b4f82a7:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca1072fce0f2aca8" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca1072fce0f2aca8" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca1072fce0f2aca8:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:54b31db724d5aacc --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca1072fce0f2aca8:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:708bbea1a18df245 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca1072fce0f2aca8:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:ee8dcdcf90472dbd --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca1072fce0f2aca8:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:1027138ad4233ee2 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca1072fce0f2aca8:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:6bff867beee968b9 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca1072fce0f2aca8:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:31eb4220eea607fc --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca1072fce0f2aca8:2:1">女灶神</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca1072fce0f2aca8:2:2">丹阳</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca1072fce0f2aca8:2:3">小拉菲改</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca1072fce0f2aca8:2:4">夕立改</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca1072fce0f2aca8:2:5">镇海</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca1072fce0f2aca8:2:6">杰维斯</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
