<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:688360d11cd5970a" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:688360d11cd5970a" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:688360d11cd5970a:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:3ad6f5319599caa9 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:688360d11cd5970a:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:59dcd872e3d0bf50 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:688360d11cd5970a:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:030115dada625fc6 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:688360d11cd5970a:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:1abd90823749868b --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:688360d11cd5970a:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:0a093dcee2c92e10 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:688360d11cd5970a:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:b9e379085597c4e4 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:688360d11cd5970a:2:1">女灶神</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:688360d11cd5970a:2:2">Z1改</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:688360d11cd5970a:2:3">小拉菲改</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:688360d11cd5970a:2:4">绫波改</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:688360d11cd5970a:2:5">镇海</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:688360d11cd5970a:2:6">夕立改</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:211:src-7d080e8d651b:table:688360d11cd5970a:table -->

<!-- source-body:212:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:8ad7955a300356f9:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:non-prose -->
特别备注：
<!-- source-body:213:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:2a7a2080607169c0:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
小拉菲：金防空锁死，可带对空雷达（苍蝇拍）视索敌情况0-2个。示例如图：
<!-- source-body:214:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:4dd1ed163c71f9ff:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:215:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:b39a31a0ed470688:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
丹阳：不刚需金防空，单体大招收益没有那么高，根据个人实际选择防空炮。305和e1优先级差不多，但不推荐命中过低的组合，均衡搭配即可。示例如图：
<!-- source-body:216:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:d0c7248165f706b9:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:217:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:47d826f1adf49ca7:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
女灶神和镇海前文提过，不再重复。
<!-- source-body:218:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:e0b594c421299f9c:paragraph -->

### 2.金中船
<!-- source-body:219:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:26c2eb23d5ee6377:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
防守索敌线以曾经的主流三轻利根（欧根旗）为基准约在1520左右，由于索敌飞机的因素，稳健点可以保持1600以上索敌。当前版本二四轻母的场合没什么特别优势。
<!-- source-body:220:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:0ffba077602059ea:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
装甲类型为中轻甲，因此雷击鱼雷输出较弱，炮击航空表现好。
<!-- source-body:221:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:e80d782875d8d02e:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
除特别说明外，默认阵型推荐复纵。
<!-- source-body:222:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:8156f3a6c98e2dd9:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:223:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:06767941995c6f26:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
参考阵容
<!-- source-body:224:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:c97df0780ddb8856:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
目前比较多样化，没有特别标准的答案。
<!-- source-body:225:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:2ce49429b26c98d9:paragraph -->

#### ①凤栖队
<!-- source-body:226:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:91f544e44a5c3c3e:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
只推荐如下一种配队方式
<!-- source-body:227:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:9958be33f4d2e626:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
优点：几乎全能不被克制，不怕T劣，一键清屏。
<!-- source-body:228:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:d5b9cb8968123d8c:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
缺点：造价极其昂贵，三信物+改造；遇见轻母排头易出现随机数后撤罚站输掉。
<!-- source-body:229:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:cc97100ed3b30f50:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:729244af7c6ec073" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:729244af7c6ec073" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:729244af7c6ec073:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:fab6e72f62885f79 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:729244af7c6ec073:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:91280a95e074d843 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:729244af7c6ec073:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:30064c5f311fd2d2 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:729244af7c6ec073:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:dfece1a1b200c741 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:729244af7c6ec073:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:562d9d02b3d41f78 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:729244af7c6ec073:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:da9450bbe14740ab --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:729244af7c6ec073:2:1">平海</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:729244af7c6ec073:2:2">应瑞</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:729244af7c6ec073:2:3">重庆</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:729244af7c6ec073:2:4">逸仙</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:729244af7c6ec073:2:5">海伦娜改</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:729244af7c6ec073:2:6">宁海</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:230:src-7d080e8d651b:table:729244af7c6ec073:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
可根据环境给平海一个机动装置。
<!-- source-body:231:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:137b48a52a4fc595:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:232:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:11d11190b05bfd2b:paragraph -->

#### ②高索敌T优队
<!-- source-body:233:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:537bea895c3bdd38:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
以利根旗舰和诺福克旗舰增加索敌特性，来达成大概率T优效果。
<!-- source-body:234:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:42bff0db02f06886:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:06c6bebd5363c454" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:06c6bebd5363c454" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:06c6bebd5363c454:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:c75c2cdcfa8912c5 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:06c6bebd5363c454:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:117bd07d4e9a5b80 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:06c6bebd5363c454:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:c77c6f853c15b8dc --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:06c6bebd5363c454:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:43b6361dc3635f50 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:06c6bebd5363c454:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:7f37a5c2f916f173 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:06c6bebd5363c454:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:84c08ce2650ba8f3 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:06c6bebd5363c454:2:1">隼鹰</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:06c6bebd5363c454:2:2">飞鹰改</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:06c6bebd5363c454:2:3">利根</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:06c6bebd5363c454:2:4">平海</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:06c6bebd5363c454:2:5">宁海</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:06c6bebd5363c454:2:6">埃罗芒什</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:235:src-7d080e8d651b:table:06c6bebd5363c454:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑加上索敌SBD可以达到2500左右的索敌，能稳定T优绝大多数队伍。
<!-- source-body:236:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:0f0794b6db7d015c:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
优点：进攻几乎不会翻车，防守也能吃分。
<!-- source-body:237:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:3e91f6b78c27670f:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
缺点：非T优等于输，比较怕防空队。
<!-- source-body:238:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:8fc097d767f721fe:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:63e779fa594e6b00" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:63e779fa594e6b00" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:63e779fa594e6b00:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d17171d8f6e208e6 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:63e779fa594e6b00:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:f0060592d61feba2 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:63e779fa594e6b00:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:9f680d1a07193399 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:63e779fa594e6b00:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:9683964a3c0c3037 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:63e779fa594e6b00:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:33f36802b9ab09d9 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:63e779fa594e6b00:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:fe49977c4eb2acac --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:63e779fa594e6b00:2:1">飞鹰改</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:63e779fa594e6b00:2:2">隼鹰</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:63e779fa594e6b00:2:3">利根</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:63e779fa594e6b00:2:4">黑暗界</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:63e779fa594e6b00:2:5">平海</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:63e779fa594e6b00:2:6">宁海</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:239:src-7d080e8d651b:table:63e779fa594e6b00:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑专门用来打凤栖队的特化，面对其它队伍不是很强。
<!-- source-body:240:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:1b93689d703c3224:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:10ed43c25c5ec464" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:10ed43c25c5ec464" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:10ed43c25c5ec464:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:64f0f8510656be1c --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:10ed43c25c5ec464:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:ac68039b920c0ba5 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:10ed43c25c5ec464:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:437d1e273ec2c464 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:10ed43c25c5ec464:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:6df554a5059f9955 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:10ed43c25c5ec464:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:2117fc9e177f7b35 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:10ed43c25c5ec464:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:06e9e9425a5f686c --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:10ed43c25c5ec464:2:1">诺福克</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:10ed43c25c5ec464:2:2">大淀</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:10ed43c25c5ec464:2:3">加里波第</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:10ed43c25c5ec464:2:4">平海</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:10ed43c25c5ec464:2:5">海伦娜改</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:10ed43c25c5ec464:2:6">宁海</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:241:src-7d080e8d651b:table:10ed43c25c5ec464:table -->

<!-- source-body:242:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:86da190f67fe1d82:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:c06c782993d800a7" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:c06c782993d800a7" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:c06c782993d800a7:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:53dd816939585c53 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:c06c782993d800a7:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:874396475085cda6 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:c06c782993d800a7:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:1077619efc0df493 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:c06c782993d800a7:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:ba19917d7b1a7af8 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:c06c782993d800a7:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:2df71ade3dc3595c --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:c06c782993d800a7:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:53884fd3fcc3096c --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:c06c782993d800a7:2:1">隼鹰</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:c06c782993d800a7:2:2">诺福克</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:c06c782993d800a7:2:3">平海</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:c06c782993d800a7:2:4">海伦娜改</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:c06c782993d800a7:2:5">宁海</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:c06c782993d800a7:2:6">大淀</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:243:src-7d080e8d651b:table:c06c782993d800a7:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
优点：综合性略强，防守也能吃一些分。
<!-- source-body:244:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a9ffff3aa34d6017:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
缺点：面对传统欧根旗三轻容易翻车，索敌低未必能t优。
<!-- source-body:245:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:0f84abdcdeb71068:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:246:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:885ef3e4a10af3b0:paragraph -->

#### ③单轻母
<!-- source-body:247:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:d28a891335751a75:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
以航空护盾为核心的队伍，以及此基础上产生的多种变阵。阵型复纵
<!-- source-body:248:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:36353ff9e3de9da1:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:74cce592a3c056a2" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:74cce592a3c056a2" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:74cce592a3c056a2:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:10721bb42c09f1fe --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:74cce592a3c056a2:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:eba90b56362455d4 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:74cce592a3c056a2:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:e85b338cdce1fefa --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:74cce592a3c056a2:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:71aa17bd21f6ccf2 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:74cce592a3c056a2:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:03f826e5da20ea1d --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:74cce592a3c056a2:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:f200190eb00cce30 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:74cce592a3c056a2:2:1">隼鹰</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:74cce592a3c056a2:2:2">圣地亚哥</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:74cce592a3c056a2:2:3">明尼</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:74cce592a3c056a2:2:4">贝法</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:74cce592a3c056a2:2:5">欧根</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:74cce592a3c056a2:2:6">波拉</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:249:src-7d080e8d651b:table:74cce592a3c056a2:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
优点：克制三轻母利根队，生存能力强，防守表现不错。
<!-- source-body:250:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:d468b393207ed6c8:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
缺点：伤害较低，面对T优索敌队和炮击队有些乏力。
<!-- source-body:251:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:98bed2b539978142:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:252:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:1a892aca81584cff:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
在满足索敌需求上，可根据需求进行变阵：
<!-- source-body:253:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:b89a577c24c9e8e3:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（1）常见定位相近的舰灵：输出位波拉|黑暗界，重庆|宁海|逸仙；辅助位贝法|海伦娜|大淀|圣地亚哥（金坷垃）；生存位爱宕|加里波第|明尼。
<!-- source-body:254:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:0ec000e099a6610e:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
（2）常用搭配：金坷垃+明尼；黑暗界+海伦娜；大淀+加里波第；重庆+海伦娜等等。
<!-- source-body:255:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:d3d4d3d32ff26935:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:non-prose -->
示例：
<!-- source-body:256:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:b823664f46b3af3d:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2eae4f1f27368aaa" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2eae4f1f27368aaa" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2eae4f1f27368aaa:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:4dafdb9f60d20a38 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2eae4f1f27368aaa:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:9f0347d54e22e9e5 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2eae4f1f27368aaa:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:ae78b2b06588b288 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2eae4f1f27368aaa:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:5ed7700247fd6b2e --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2eae4f1f27368aaa:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:f5a6e825079c60c5 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2eae4f1f27368aaa:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:2f1db89adcd6b567 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2eae4f1f27368aaa:2:1">隼鹰</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2eae4f1f27368aaa:2:2">大淀</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2eae4f1f27368aaa:2:3">加里波第</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2eae4f1f27368aaa:2:4">欧根</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2eae4f1f27368aaa:2:5">海伦娜改</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2eae4f1f27368aaa:2:6">宁海</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:257:src-7d080e8d651b:table:2eae4f1f27368aaa:table -->

<!-- source-body:258:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:eb76cced0b0b9071:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b19007b1bc2047f6" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b19007b1bc2047f6" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b19007b1bc2047f6:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:7d257da52a828e30 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b19007b1bc2047f6:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:00b98d46a76cad12 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b19007b1bc2047f6:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d5b6b1e095b4a95b --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b19007b1bc2047f6:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:683d874f6f5a65e0 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b19007b1bc2047f6:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:e4e75b7d9ea355cb --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b19007b1bc2047f6:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:a766ec621e138ded --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b19007b1bc2047f6:2:1">隼鹰</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b19007b1bc2047f6:2:2">加里波第</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b19007b1bc2047f6:2:3">明尼</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b19007b1bc2047f6:2:4">大淀</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b19007b1bc2047f6:2:5">贝法</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b19007b1bc2047f6:2:6">欧根</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:259:src-7d080e8d651b:table:b19007b1bc2047f6:table -->

<!-- source-body:260:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:8e9600d77aed7c86:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:5e6fa419374e3e83" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:5e6fa419374e3e83" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:5e6fa419374e3e83:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:e8b9d2db1279766c --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:5e6fa419374e3e83:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:e17919f2b1aea413 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:5e6fa419374e3e83:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:3fa8d83a9a5f1dda --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:5e6fa419374e3e83:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:1156e8a8ebb93dd2 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:5e6fa419374e3e83:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:388fcb50d1b3032b --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:5e6fa419374e3e83:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:036e2c7616badbd5 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:5e6fa419374e3e83:2:1">隼鹰</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:5e6fa419374e3e83:2:2">海伦娜改</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:5e6fa419374e3e83:2:3">爱宕</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:5e6fa419374e3e83:2:4">欧根</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:5e6fa419374e3e83:2:5">黑暗界</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:5e6fa419374e3e83:2:6">波拉</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:261:src-7d080e8d651b:table:5e6fa419374e3e83:table -->

#### ④三轻利根队（欧根旗）
<!-- source-body:262:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:0a3f47877e789dc0:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
曾经使用较久和广泛的主流队伍。阵型轮型/复纵
<!-- source-body:263:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:52b8d8040d2d3b4c:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
优点：培养成本低，战斗力不错。
<!-- source-body:264:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:9c28f1d8970ec4a6:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
缺点：现环境轻母略疲软。
<!-- source-body:265:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:1f32b6e4e30fc909:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b27130fb7502fa0e" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b27130fb7502fa0e" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b27130fb7502fa0e:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:ffb86ef1c17b1a26 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b27130fb7502fa0e:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:5c781f725ebec0bc --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b27130fb7502fa0e:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:58ed78004d5fcd9e --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b27130fb7502fa0e:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:c94353aff66a67d7 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b27130fb7502fa0e:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:e2fab9ca8d143e17 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b27130fb7502fa0e:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d5493ab22a2f730f --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b27130fb7502fa0e:2:1">隼鹰</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b27130fb7502fa0e:2:2">飞鹰改</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b27130fb7502fa0e:2:3">明尼</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b27130fb7502fa0e:2:4">利根</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b27130fb7502fa0e:2:5">欧根</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b27130fb7502fa0e:2:6">埃罗芒什</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:266:src-7d080e8d651b:table:b27130fb7502fa0e:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
同航：全索敌，多上索敌飞机争取T优。
<!-- source-body:267:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:e922fd1c5abb66e2:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
反航：多用金剑鱼；利根因为没有中减，又是buff船，生存可以一到二机动。明尼欧根可使用对空雷达（苍蝇拍）
<!-- source-body:268:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:cb8b189d7b12d628:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:269:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a8c26eff4e03dc9c:paragraph -->

#### ⑤零轻母炮击队
<!-- source-body:270:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:4fc9a04eb6aa3d10:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
完全舍弃轻母，纯正的炮击队伍，目前炮击旗舰仍较弱，还是推荐减伤旗舰。有多种变阵请自行调整（原理同单轻母）。索敌足够时可增加一个机动装置。
<!-- source-body:271:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:06b3ce31de7d8008:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
优点：相对克制单轻母
<!-- source-body:272:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a6251a771cbe3b9d:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
缺点：命中低，索敌低
<!-- source-body:273:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:fdfe29e0492f3c40:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:45f2ba01cc4fe5b7" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:45f2ba01cc4fe5b7" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:45f2ba01cc4fe5b7:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:970065a9b2154697 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:45f2ba01cc4fe5b7:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:8464182e5c8aeebe --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:45f2ba01cc4fe5b7:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:e611b3dacfe68634 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:45f2ba01cc4fe5b7:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:bec0a2b7c1cc4dd2 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:45f2ba01cc4fe5b7:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:59ece0fc6982ab53 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:45f2ba01cc4fe5b7:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:6c02fcbbb611c70d --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:45f2ba01cc4fe5b7:2:1">诺福克</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:45f2ba01cc4fe5b7:2:2">大淀</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:45f2ba01cc4fe5b7:2:3">加里波第</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:45f2ba01cc4fe5b7:2:4">欧根</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:45f2ba01cc4fe5b7:2:5">海伦娜改</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:45f2ba01cc4fe5b7:2:6">宁海</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:274:src-7d080e8d651b:table:45f2ba01cc4fe5b7:table -->

<!-- source-body:275:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:77e8de47b9145366:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:09807bb37e59a9b0" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:09807bb37e59a9b0" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:09807bb37e59a9b0:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:441f3d928d6e1842 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:09807bb37e59a9b0:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:3edcb83f714f8d8d --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:09807bb37e59a9b0:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:af62490f37be5f73 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:09807bb37e59a9b0:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:06a34a47d8ed57d6 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:09807bb37e59a9b0:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:9f016c7790a49430 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:09807bb37e59a9b0:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d2abe34871f67b1f --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:09807bb37e59a9b0:2:1">诺福克</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:09807bb37e59a9b0:2:2">大淀</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:09807bb37e59a9b0:2:3">加里波第</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:09807bb37e59a9b0:2:4">海伦娜改</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:09807bb37e59a9b0:2:5">黑暗界</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:09807bb37e59a9b0:2:6">欧根</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:276:src-7d080e8d651b:table:09807bb37e59a9b0:table -->

<!-- source-body:277:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:d0996419c20f409e:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d085264e76d12a3e" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d085264e76d12a3e" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d085264e76d12a3e:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:ad00fe57fb07ae77 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d085264e76d12a3e:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:0e22abf7719a6e23 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d085264e76d12a3e:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:632ee4a1ca8f5c37 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d085264e76d12a3e:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:4c15503b22149d46 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d085264e76d12a3e:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:07d51d776b390bb0 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d085264e76d12a3e:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d1f1089c25ee9bac --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d085264e76d12a3e:2:1">诺福克</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d085264e76d12a3e:2:2">圣地亚哥</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d085264e76d12a3e:2:3">加里波第</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d085264e76d12a3e:2:4">明尼</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d085264e76d12a3e:2:5">海伦娜改</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d085264e76d12a3e:2:6">欧根</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:278:src-7d080e8d651b:table:d085264e76d12a3e:table -->

<!-- source-body:279:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:51d4237e1cc10d72:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:aa4e161ef63c01eb" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:aa4e161ef63c01eb" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:aa4e161ef63c01eb:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:7e0f434851ed58ad --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:aa4e161ef63c01eb:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:0cbaf37f1a20dcc0 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:aa4e161ef63c01eb:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:cba4d016be95a710 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:aa4e161ef63c01eb:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:33398396cbb18090 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:aa4e161ef63c01eb:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:fa8f7b12fb558951 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:aa4e161ef63c01eb:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:ea26f718e066c99b --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:aa4e161ef63c01eb:2:1">萨福克</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:aa4e161ef63c01eb:2:2">大淀</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:aa4e161ef63c01eb:2:3">加里波第</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:aa4e161ef63c01eb:2:4">海伦娜改</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:aa4e161ef63c01eb:2:5">黑暗界</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:aa4e161ef63c01eb:2:6">欧根</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:280:src-7d080e8d651b:table:aa4e161ef63c01eb:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
⑥雷巡队-需要赛季buff的队伍，中规中矩。
<!-- source-body:281:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:6c730763c8157ca6:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
优点：射起来很爽。
<!-- source-body:282:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:952824af8c3f7a3a:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
缺点：三改造造价高；容易空雷。
<!-- source-body:283:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:e2a3e9116288904d:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7cfee01e605ff72e" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7cfee01e605ff72e" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7cfee01e605ff72e:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:9b67bf9744e149ab --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7cfee01e605ff72e:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:a758af4444617172 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7cfee01e605ff72e:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:887d10099228f04a --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7cfee01e605ff72e:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:a3df312cc36d4e23 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7cfee01e605ff72e:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:b76150a16a966144 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7cfee01e605ff72e:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:56c1f610ed45533e --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7cfee01e605ff72e:2:1">隼鹰</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7cfee01e605ff72e:2:2">北上改</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7cfee01e605ff72e:2:3">加里波第</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7cfee01e605ff72e:2:4">神通改</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7cfee01e605ff72e:2:5">大井改</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:7cfee01e605ff72e:2:6">欧根</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:284:src-7d080e8d651b:table:7cfee01e605ff72e:table -->

#### ⑦大淀滑板雷击队（纯娱乐）
<!-- source-body:285:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:305028eaf80f8cb6:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
依靠大淀旗舰不会T劣的特性，全员带机动装置，大井可以带一个蛋糕。
<!-- source-body:286:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:08322ab71afd830d:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
优点：进攻不错，射得爽，闪避多。
<!-- source-body:287:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f7dafa9d44dfcba7:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
缺点：造价极其昂贵；防守送对面T优buff。
<!-- source-body:288:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:71b22979c49c16f4:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:145fa9b48dde0f2b" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:145fa9b48dde0f2b" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:145fa9b48dde0f2b:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:a1512642f8b796a3 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:145fa9b48dde0f2b:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:9d4bd60120e35415 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:145fa9b48dde0f2b:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:3cb0fb39fa66b9c1 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:145fa9b48dde0f2b:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:a7fccfe14e62b9b7 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:145fa9b48dde0f2b:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:1738ae94d655d3d5 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:145fa9b48dde0f2b:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:9d026d763c40b908 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:145fa9b48dde0f2b:2:1">隼鹰</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:145fa9b48dde0f2b:2:2">大淀</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:145fa9b48dde0f2b:2:3">北上改</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:145fa9b48dde0f2b:2:4">神通改</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:145fa9b48dde0f2b:2:5">大井改</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:145fa9b48dde0f2b:2:6">宁海</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:289:src-7d080e8d651b:table:145fa9b48dde0f2b:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:non-prose -->
特别备注：
<!-- source-body:290:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:baebcf3846df7c3a:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
加里波第因为有巨额炮击buff，优选高炮击数值装备。示例：
<!-- source-body:291:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:cc427771bf633d64:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:292:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:eddfba6eafbe74a6:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
宁海输出主要来自龙拳，优先非常多的命中装备而不是炮击。此外机动也可以增加输出，酌情调整。示例：
<!-- source-body:293:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:169e29a54d65a77d:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:294:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:446c47da7c5484a0:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
北上在大淀滑板队中，经计算，天赋点机动的收益比减伤要高。
<!-- source-body:295:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:8821004a7bc8b867:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:296:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:d4b4e35dd8a89377:paragraph -->

### 3.金大船
<!-- source-body:297:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:af80e1930ee08a99:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
大船队伍互相之间有克制，可谓百花齐放，当期版本练航队终于不再是上当受骗。当前版本虽有纯航队的高索敌2300+，但一般进攻时会低一些。推荐防守索敌线1650左右，最好不要低于1600。速成推荐混编3+3，即三航三炮的队伍。航队造价整体偏高，美系偏轰炸，日系偏鱼雷。
<!-- source-body:298:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a1c2321dddb79599:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
金场的战列系和装母为重甲，只有航母是中甲。因此往往穿甲战列和鱼雷航母优先级高，高爆战列和轰航选择较少。
<!-- source-body:299:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:c37d5d8618963696:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
常用搭配：黎北前，吃喝，vv天鹰，列萨等
<!-- source-body:300:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:39bc8d6e221d7d88:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:301:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:dd0cc783c695e271:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
参考阵容
<!-- source-body:302:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:2d69a505e8f2b896:paragraph -->

#### ①VV旗
<!-- source-body:303:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:ee61f951ac10ea05:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
以维内托改旗舰的高伤害自爆流。阵型选择梯型阵。
<!-- source-body:304:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:8222b0e1b446b694:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
优点：非常通用，不容易被克制，防守也好赢。
<!-- source-body:305:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:81e439bfa3acfaa5:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
缺点：太吃随机数，不稳定。
<!-- source-body:306:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:31e0b99fc54ce5a5:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d46dffe37e674dc3" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d46dffe37e674dc3" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d46dffe37e674dc3:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:635ef294599c0e09 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d46dffe37e674dc3:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:90ba9b07af413002 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d46dffe37e674dc3:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:87814ce5825ff9f5 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d46dffe37e674dc3:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:251d9a039bb45f18 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d46dffe37e674dc3:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:47409bda0390b2a6 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d46dffe37e674dc3:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:ed08a3ba4874ea7d --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d46dffe37e674dc3:2:1">维内托改</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d46dffe37e674dc3:2:2">大凤改</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d46dffe37e674dc3:2:3">北卡</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d46dffe37e674dc3:2:4">前卫</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d46dffe37e674dc3:2:5">赤城</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d46dffe37e674dc3:2:6">加贺</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:307:src-7d080e8d651b:table:d46dffe37e674dc3:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑老式全索敌
<!-- source-body:308:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:163e8c1e5a457766:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:309:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:2faa3d69bab418b5:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca5c698b6b3d3dcd" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca5c698b6b3d3dcd" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca5c698b6b3d3dcd:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:4ea5a0a9f3e1ded9 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca5c698b6b3d3dcd:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:679034ebd461c7d5 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca5c698b6b3d3dcd:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:db2e5a711f5e7b57 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca5c698b6b3d3dcd:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:1e1222a73e36c8bc --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca5c698b6b3d3dcd:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:70425efe9116c10c --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca5c698b6b3d3dcd:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:a1606934caac86cf --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca5c698b6b3d3dcd:2:1">天鹰</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca5c698b6b3d3dcd:2:2">北卡</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca5c698b6b3d3dcd:2:3">维内托改</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca5c698b6b3d3dcd:2:4">赤城</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca5c698b6b3d3dcd:2:5">加贺</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca5c698b6b3d3dcd:2:6">前卫</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:310:src-7d080e8d651b:table:ca5c698b6b3d3dcd:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑反航场合个人推荐T位带1-2机动装置。
<!-- source-body:311:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:b51f5f47ce70fa3b:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:be87bffacf8a819a" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:be87bffacf8a819a" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:be87bffacf8a819a:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:59a1a60056b21bdf --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:be87bffacf8a819a:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:712d4d81f5e30e10 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:be87bffacf8a819a:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:6ccbfe0045e8f64d --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:be87bffacf8a819a:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:41d1cfbe52761358 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:be87bffacf8a819a:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:c49a7473c3cdb26c --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:be87bffacf8a819a:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:802e19fda51d5fd7 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:be87bffacf8a819a:2:1">维内托改</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:be87bffacf8a819a:2:2">天鹰</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:be87bffacf8a819a:2:3">北卡</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:be87bffacf8a819a:2:4">赤城</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:be87bffacf8a819a:2:5">加贺</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:be87bffacf8a819a:2:6">前卫</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:312:src-7d080e8d651b:table:be87bffacf8a819a:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
这种站位也较常见，vv可带普列赛。
<!-- source-body:313:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:65d2ecef1234246e:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:314:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:cf981266e505ae88:paragraph -->

#### ②墩旗
<!-- source-body:315:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:a640a628aa6085e4:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
数值强大的体现，35炮击减伤。
<!-- source-body:316:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:d851fa46462bc6d9:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
优点：面对炮击伤害几乎免疫，较为通用。
<!-- source-body:317:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:7bd74d3bacc4b45a:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
缺点：被航队克制，索敌相对低。
<!-- source-body:318:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:99303b498c899ebb:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:228a494552beb536" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:228a494552beb536" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:228a494552beb536:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:88adc71de6eadb74 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:228a494552beb536:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:2fc6a4b96984cda8 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:228a494552beb536:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:51714b3d70096a76 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:228a494552beb536:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:1fe2e9093b3c3f7e --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:228a494552beb536:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:41eba054d35cfc1a --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:228a494552beb536:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:262aad594ed57014 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:228a494552beb536:2:1">天鹰</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:228a494552beb536:2:2">前卫</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:228a494552beb536:2:3">北卡</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:228a494552beb536:2:4">赤城</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:228a494552beb536:2:5">加贺</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:228a494552beb536:2:6">敦刻尔克</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:319:src-7d080e8d651b:table:228a494552beb536:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑推荐新3+3版，适应性更强，阵型单纵。T位可带两个滑板，此时复纵阵。
<!-- source-body:320:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:001dc23adcc81b01:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2e7e5bd885ac2622" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2e7e5bd885ac2622" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2e7e5bd885ac2622:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d329616f2f971949 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2e7e5bd885ac2622:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:52e49ec0f8b6db6d --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2e7e5bd885ac2622:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:32cbc6be19137cd7 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2e7e5bd885ac2622:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:4c5bc7e4e0c39cda --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2e7e5bd885ac2622:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:b0ff6c49ddae6e80 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2e7e5bd885ac2622:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:254f5947ccd86780 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2e7e5bd885ac2622:2:1">黎塞留改</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2e7e5bd885ac2622:2:2">前卫</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2e7e5bd885ac2622:2:3">北卡</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2e7e5bd885ac2622:2:4">赤城</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2e7e5bd885ac2622:2:5">加贺</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:2e7e5bd885ac2622:2:6">敦刻尔克</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:321:src-7d080e8d651b:table:2e7e5bd885ac2622:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑老版本4+2，黎塞留可带1-2个普列赛。阵型单纵阵
<!-- source-body:322:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:40809dc59395d752:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:323:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:4e3110f8a5691e4b:paragraph -->

#### ③前卫旗
<!-- source-body:324:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:7c38ffe58dcd51fa:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
推荐全索敌，轮型阵。
<!-- source-body:325:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:d6b7e60c2e535c79:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
优点：克制航队，面对3+3表现也不错，较为通用。
<!-- source-body:326:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:6aeb8a8fa6641913:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
缺点：吃喝被炮击容易暴毙。
<!-- source-body:327:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:b24db304a92bceca:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:332:src-7d080e8d651b:table:10de91e6f7a60210:table -->

<!-- source-body:333:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f772cc2c32eb8d42:paragraph -->

#### ④英队
<!-- source-body:334:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:79c906f11432dce0:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
英国国家队体系。个人不推荐带皇家方舟，带了也难过墩子，打别人还容易翻车。
<!-- source-body:335:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:5cdb096d08b594d1:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d0f3d23bd31e7a92" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d0f3d23bd31e7a92" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d0f3d23bd31e7a92:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:48b7ef6c2384cbbd --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d0f3d23bd31e7a92:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:864df1839e343efd --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d0f3d23bd31e7a92:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:85052eae72eab97a --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d0f3d23bd31e7a92:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:addeaa891280ae10 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d0f3d23bd31e7a92:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d7dd258a5d93dbce --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d0f3d23bd31e7a92:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:907f87facad91497 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d0f3d23bd31e7a92:2:1">约克公爵</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d0f3d23bd31e7a92:2:2">天鹰</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d0f3d23bd31e7a92:2:3">光辉</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d0f3d23bd31e7a92:2:4">前卫</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d0f3d23bd31e7a92:2:5">胡德改</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d0f3d23bd31e7a92:2:6">伊丽莎白</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:336:src-7d080e8d651b:table:d0f3d23bd31e7a92:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑光辉和公爵可交换，推荐一号位克上一到两个机动装置，有伊丽莎白旗舰机动收益高。阵型复纵阵。
<!-- source-body:337:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f8f6d1ebfe0cb8a9:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
优点：通用稳定，整体肉度高，不容易出现后排暴毙。
<!-- source-body:338:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:6e10ea49f2e9a9ad:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
缺点：索敌较低；被墩子完克。
<!-- source-body:339:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:3aaf68e40323f8a9:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:340:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:eb74938747d8d848:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
偶尔可尝试的变种英队
<!-- source-body:341:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:2d002c4e31b5f37c:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:8dc3a3907c7fc4cd" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:8dc3a3907c7fc4cd" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:8dc3a3907c7fc4cd:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:48f2141db8c71ba4 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:8dc3a3907c7fc4cd:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:373e1bd14573403c --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:8dc3a3907c7fc4cd:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:a8dcd38277d20b02 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:8dc3a3907c7fc4cd:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:411ed12128119d9a --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:8dc3a3907c7fc4cd:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:7472f1c1f8fd860a --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:8dc3a3907c7fc4cd:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:c9765f2f48acf8fc --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:8dc3a3907c7fc4cd:2:1">光辉</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:8dc3a3907c7fc4cd:2:2">天鹰</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:8dc3a3907c7fc4cd:2:3">约克公爵</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:8dc3a3907c7fc4cd:2:4">前卫</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:8dc3a3907c7fc4cd:2:5">胡德改</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:8dc3a3907c7fc4cd:2:6">埃塞克斯</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:342:src-7d080e8d651b:table:8dc3a3907c7fc4cd:table -->

<!-- source-body:343:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:07e07d668080bb63:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d37a3b147e7fbc76" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d37a3b147e7fbc76" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d37a3b147e7fbc76:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:e756ee56411b0b01 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d37a3b147e7fbc76:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:eb1713d64221e265 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d37a3b147e7fbc76:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:7ed4fa791ae5342c --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d37a3b147e7fbc76:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:fa735b6e0553a602 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d37a3b147e7fbc76:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:af30089ae99bf516 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d37a3b147e7fbc76:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:42aaa0db17a283bd --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d37a3b147e7fbc76:2:1">光辉</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d37a3b147e7fbc76:2:2">伊丽莎白</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d37a3b147e7fbc76:2:3">约克公爵</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d37a3b147e7fbc76:2:4">前卫</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d37a3b147e7fbc76:2:5">胡德改</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:d37a3b147e7fbc76:2:6">皇家方舟</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:344:src-7d080e8d651b:table:d37a3b147e7fbc76:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
专门应付航母环境。轮形阵
<!-- source-body:345:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:6278c50268fa5f18:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:346:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:dca632801f31f4b4:paragraph -->

#### ⑤天鹰旗
<!-- source-body:347:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:4da1af69621b518e:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
可以说是航队的VV，突出一个自爆，强度很不错，较为推荐。推荐轮型阵|复纵阵。
<!-- source-body:348:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:2195c7dbf65f58e5:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
优点：伤害高清场快，甚至不惧前卫旗。
<!-- source-body:349:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:357e9046e2bd21a0:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
缺点：遇见同行无法保证胜率，面对轰航死路一条
<!-- source-body:350:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:dd1d2bdc500d363d:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3dcd6a7d35028e78" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3dcd6a7d35028e78" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3dcd6a7d35028e78:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:199dbc4860484bae --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3dcd6a7d35028e78:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:b9f9aa040c4fc8ac --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3dcd6a7d35028e78:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:17eaa4219af38918 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3dcd6a7d35028e78:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:7b9fae3ad9947f0d --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3dcd6a7d35028e78:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:2bb451c6ed57a6eb --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3dcd6a7d35028e78:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:0d1df1c3307be831 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3dcd6a7d35028e78:2:1">光辉</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3dcd6a7d35028e78:2:2">埃塞克斯</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3dcd6a7d35028e78:2:3">大凤改</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3dcd6a7d35028e78:2:4">赤城</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3dcd6a7d35028e78:2:5">加贺</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3dcd6a7d35028e78:2:6">天鹰</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:351:src-7d080e8d651b:table:3dcd6a7d35028e78:table -->

<!-- source-body:352:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:33e6938152e93960:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:1a1722bf847eb034" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:1a1722bf847eb034" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:1a1722bf847eb034:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:a60f20579aef8835 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:1a1722bf847eb034:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:cf8a2b7b411db34a --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:1a1722bf847eb034:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:f67814ee2bec5f8d --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:1a1722bf847eb034:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:19b3a9e767dd361f --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:1a1722bf847eb034:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:a247198de089357b --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:1a1722bf847eb034:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d9b01317c7a6b885 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:1a1722bf847eb034:2:1">大凤改</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:1a1722bf847eb034:2:2">埃塞克斯</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:1a1722bf847eb034:2:3">北卡</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:1a1722bf847eb034:2:4">赤城</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:1a1722bf847eb034:2:5">加贺</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:1a1722bf847eb034:2:6">天鹰</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:353:src-7d080e8d651b:table:1a1722bf847eb034:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑更适合航队内战，有天鹰buff和北卡双重保障。
<!-- source-body:354:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:ebde4744a5da4db8:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:355:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:47b52a787fdacec1:paragraph -->

#### ⑥日航（机动航）
<!-- source-body:356:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:2b6e8c43e288b32e:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
曾经强势，现在也不错，个人推荐带航战。阵型复纵阵。
<!-- source-body:357:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:31e584644028a1f1:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
进攻需多带命中飞机、金剑鱼和机动装置，索敌要1600+；防守尽量全烈风全天河抬高索敌线至2000以上。
<!-- source-body:358:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:73258950a5c1202c:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
优点：生存不错，较通用，面对前卫旗也有一战之力。
<!-- source-body:359:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:9b366be2481b9598:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
缺点：装备需求高，伤害不高容易进夜战；被轰航完克
<!-- source-body:360:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:111330cbe5421c7e:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:049e04a2a7e1899b" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:049e04a2a7e1899b" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:049e04a2a7e1899b:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:8a9114df06797faa --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:049e04a2a7e1899b:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:bdf53f791f6e47b6 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:049e04a2a7e1899b:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:b14b81afe67a6c65 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:049e04a2a7e1899b:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:a3d90f3a24a5aee6 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:049e04a2a7e1899b:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:982f56ea0124b8bb --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:049e04a2a7e1899b:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:6250dc3f317eb670 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:049e04a2a7e1899b:2:1">翔鹤</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:049e04a2a7e1899b:2:2">大凤改</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:049e04a2a7e1899b:2:3">苍龙</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:049e04a2a7e1899b:2:4">赤城</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:049e04a2a7e1899b:2:5">加贺</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:049e04a2a7e1899b:2:6">瑞鹤</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:361:src-7d080e8d651b:table:049e04a2a7e1899b:table -->

<!-- source-body:362:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:cbd9f1cc8bfc338d:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa2276061f816cb" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa2276061f816cb" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa2276061f816cb:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:841cf96224060691 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa2276061f816cb:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:59fdcf9dfef013b7 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa2276061f816cb:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:dc44af27344a6246 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa2276061f816cb:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:8f4f66e5529f5c90 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa2276061f816cb:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d8a2ffe13e2e0000 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa2276061f816cb:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:67364c9b4aaca756 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa2276061f816cb:2:1">翔鹤</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa2276061f816cb:2:2">天鹰</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa2276061f816cb:2:3">苍龙</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa2276061f816cb:2:4">加贺</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa2276061f816cb:2:5">赤城</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa2276061f816cb:2:6">瑞鹤</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:363:src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa2276061f816cb:table -->

<!-- source-body:364:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:c60ae9fc66081283:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166c42b05d28373f" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166c42b05d28373f" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166c42b05d28373f:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:7418f937f4604ded --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166c42b05d28373f:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:c2d5fc4ae8a8c6e3 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166c42b05d28373f:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:8b5cf302b85a3e1d --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166c42b05d28373f:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:2f941cd755aabbeb --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166c42b05d28373f:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:8ed9737b88a95ea6 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166c42b05d28373f:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:21e77f651a52f452 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166c42b05d28373f:2:1">翔鹤</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166c42b05d28373f:2:2">伊势改</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166c42b05d28373f:2:3">日向改</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166c42b05d28373f:2:4">瑞鹤</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166c42b05d28373f:2:5">赤城</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:166c42b05d28373f:2:6">加贺</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:365:src-7d080e8d651b:table:166c42b05d28373f:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑航战点回避天赋，增加了内战胜率和夜战容错。这个阵容翔鹤必须两机动，瑞鹤旗舰全靠她
<!-- source-body:366:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:8b97e9914a30c803:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:367:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:cc5c7d629734dde5:paragraph -->

#### ⑦美队
<!-- source-body:368:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:c1107c1ffab9b680:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
南达科他信物为核心的美队体系，非常推荐,飞机优先爆伤飞机。阵型梯形阵。
<!-- source-body:369:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a536777556713d91:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
优点：伤害肉度均衡，适应性自由度高，不怕暴毙容错高
<!-- source-body:370:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:efd8ba452225979f:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
缺点：减员能力不如吃喝前；舰灵不常用，需额外养成
<!-- source-body:371:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:650a2af29193548f:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bf2d9b720c4d8642" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bf2d9b720c4d8642" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bf2d9b720c4d8642:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:5c3ef5d9c8829d39 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bf2d9b720c4d8642:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:b9ee528cb3ca2971 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bf2d9b720c4d8642:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:cbd684071912e8dc --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bf2d9b720c4d8642:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:51f7deb068188809 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bf2d9b720c4d8642:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d29974c6a7d058b3 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bf2d9b720c4d8642:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:da51bacb4d6fddec --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bf2d9b720c4d8642:2:1">南达科他</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bf2d9b720c4d8642:2:2">企业</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bf2d9b720c4d8642:2:3">华盛顿</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bf2d9b720c4d8642:2:4">北卡</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bf2d9b720c4d8642:2:5">约克城</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bf2d9b720c4d8642:2:6">埃塞克斯</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:372:src-7d080e8d651b:table:bf2d9b720c4d8642:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑美式3+3，华盛顿加强后会更上一层
<!-- source-body:373:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f42bb9ba940b96ca:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ff93b81fce21cfe6" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ff93b81fce21cfe6" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ff93b81fce21cfe6:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:ad6031966fe01ff0 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ff93b81fce21cfe6:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:24542fd50713ee85 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ff93b81fce21cfe6:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:ea7a441cb9ca822e --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ff93b81fce21cfe6:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:59151b45210b6a60 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ff93b81fce21cfe6:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:9282f9eea8210325 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ff93b81fce21cfe6:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:9bb55ac63f5d23dd --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ff93b81fce21cfe6:2:1">南达科他</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ff93b81fce21cfe6:2:2">企业</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ff93b81fce21cfe6:2:3">北卡</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ff93b81fce21cfe6:2:4">萨拉托加改</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ff93b81fce21cfe6:2:5">列克星敦改</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:ff93b81fce21cfe6:2:6">埃塞克斯</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:374:src-7d080e8d651b:table:ff93b81fce21cfe6:table -->

<!-- source-body:375:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:d2920d4042eddfcf:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:202bbfd8128c4ac6" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:202bbfd8128c4ac6" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:202bbfd8128c4ac6:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:42c010979456d1e5 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:202bbfd8128c4ac6:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:1fec6156a01816dc --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:202bbfd8128c4ac6:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:0bafaf1c1c6dcd01 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:202bbfd8128c4ac6:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:4b25f0d7186d47fb --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:202bbfd8128c4ac6:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:6f747658bacf4434 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:202bbfd8128c4ac6:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:34a334b19ed6944b --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:202bbfd8128c4ac6:2:1">企业</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:202bbfd8128c4ac6:2:2">齐柏林改</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:202bbfd8128c4ac6:2:3">南达科他</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:202bbfd8128c4ac6:2:4">埃塞克斯</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:202bbfd8128c4ac6:2:5">列克星敦改</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:202bbfd8128c4ac6:2:6">萨拉托加改</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:376:src-7d080e8d651b:table:202bbfd8128c4ac6:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑美轰机动航，可用梯形阵|复纵阵
<!-- source-body:377:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:b02ba926f0d1ef7a:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑老版本的全索敌流，SBD索敌飞机5个以上可以玩。刚需T优|航队环境，否则极其不推荐。阵型复纵阵。
<!-- source-body:387:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:43c404e5ca5fc50a:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:388:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:25d62857596ab9b4:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:389:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:92276aeaa50e9d9e:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:non-prose -->
特别备注：
<!-- source-body:390:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a6806a5bb0a0c58a:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
前卫大人无需多言，通常要给最好的装备。示例：
<!-- source-body:391:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:ff9a4a2b6d4acd75:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:392:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:4685de732d930005:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
