<!-- source-body:393:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f25fa609f85f48fd:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:394:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:65f2562798010037:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:395:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:1ed3a4df7d4737ba:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
南达科他，作为美系核心，信物机制决定需要两个普列塞和最高减伤装备，示例：
<!-- source-body:396:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:279964ef201b9fb9:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:397:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f747d05446c5f2bc:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
列萨推荐高爆伤飞机。示例：
<!-- source-body:398:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:78691a8047940c09:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:399:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:02e24698ca129d92:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
企业做T位的场合，推荐携带1-2机动装置，可带索敌/爆伤/幸运飞机。示例：
<!-- source-body:400:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:185fd04a86444c51:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:401:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f3c849613862a8b4:paragraph -->

## （二）主力舰队（紫场）
<!-- source-body:402:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:b33f041ccbb4e083:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
紫场的配队相对金场较少，思路原理类似，不做过多解释。
<!-- source-body:403:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:cf9cd1cd486aad68:paragraph -->

### 1.紫小船
<!-- source-body:404:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:29fc11fe89ed7845:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
推荐防守索敌线1192以上。
<!-- source-body:405:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:b0ec667bf34b8406:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
推荐舰灵：吹雪改，白雪改，信赖（响改），卡米，镇海（必带），小拉菲改，天津风，z1改。基本就在其中组合搭配。
<!-- source-body:406:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:efdda3abf9df09ce:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
阵型默认选择复纵阵。
<!-- source-body:407:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:4f85a7e70d73d449:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:408:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:5ca6e72f876f1255:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
参考阵容
<!-- source-body:409:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:5622b33248b437e4:paragraph -->

#### ①特驱队
<!-- source-body:410:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:c5359de8bd5602ec:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
白雪改旗舰提供了强大数值。游戏中特型驱逐包括：晓响雷电、四雪，改造后也算特驱。
<!-- source-body:411:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:9a9c8fa864626ee2:paragraph -->

#### ②Z1旗
<!-- source-body:421:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:2bdefedc5feafcf8:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
思路偏向进攻。
<!-- source-body:422:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:8df59568683c4da8:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b99bd17006f5a596" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b99bd17006f5a596" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b99bd17006f5a596:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:224db795daa226ca --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b99bd17006f5a596:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:6ce79f4e5fa3ff0f --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b99bd17006f5a596:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:9b67608288375974 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b99bd17006f5a596:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:5e343171cc9ef17c --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b99bd17006f5a596:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:8b8e0771e44174c2 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b99bd17006f5a596:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:deb629c56d4cffed --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b99bd17006f5a596:2:1">凉月</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b99bd17006f5a596:2:2">小拉菲改</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b99bd17006f5a596:2:3">卡米契亚</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b99bd17006f5a596:2:4">信赖</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b99bd17006f5a596:2:5">镇海</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:b99bd17006f5a596:2:6">Z1改</td></tr></tbody></table></div>

<!-- source-body:426:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:bf32474070166759:paragraph -->

### 2.紫中船
<!-- source-body:427:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:8766ff447ad44822:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
考虑索敌推荐使用诺福克旗。阵型单纵|复纵均可。
<!-- source-body:428:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:29014c1e59829281:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
参考阵容
<!-- source-body:429:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:35d3e38ab271f7ce:paragraph -->

#### ①诺福克旗
<!-- source-body:430:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:b0fbc42eca44d3fa:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
类似金场的索敌队。
<!-- source-body:431:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:010ca745404671ad:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:cf9b69b908077d53" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:cf9b69b908077d53" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:cf9b69b908077d53:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:bbc2e74d5b73d5e2 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:cf9b69b908077d53:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:516d75f58dac053e --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:cf9b69b908077d53:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:1ac3167d79740b5d --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:cf9b69b908077d53:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:8774807db3f1fd90 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:cf9b69b908077d53:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d5cce2ee4099e5c0 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:cf9b69b908077d53:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d1ede4981aebd73d --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:cf9b69b908077d53:2:1">飞鹰改</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:cf9b69b908077d53:2:2">隼鹰</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:cf9b69b908077d53:2:3">诺福克</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:cf9b69b908077d53:2:4">平海</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:cf9b69b908077d53:2:5">宁海</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:cf9b69b908077d53:2:6">独角兽</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:432:src-7d080e8d651b:table:cf9b69b908077d53:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑信物的力量，足够T优绝大多数队伍。
<!-- source-body:433:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:adb3ffb5b7155ef4:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa970049580fd8a" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa970049580fd8a" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa970049580fd8a:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:af7c4766cf92ca9f --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa970049580fd8a:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:dd3c528d7b51a698 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa970049580fd8a:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:c9f1b41636170ac2 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa970049580fd8a:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:0252761b457c6944 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa970049580fd8a:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:7018675c1b641170 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa970049580fd8a:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:bdd3455062b0d09a --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa970049580fd8a:2:1">飞鹰改</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa970049580fd8a:2:2">隼鹰</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa970049580fd8a:2:3">平海</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa970049580fd8a:2:4">诺福克</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa970049580fd8a:2:5">黑暗界</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa970049580fd8a:2:6">宁海</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:434:src-7d080e8d651b:table:0aa970049580fd8a:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑更稳健，几乎可打所有紫中队伍。
<!-- source-body:435:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f9b82864d6b208a0:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3db18122ffa03c28" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3db18122ffa03c28" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3db18122ffa03c28:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:45aeac93df6fd1df --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3db18122ffa03c28:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:1dd6fc3765f67d0e --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3db18122ffa03c28:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:64dac13598a039c4 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3db18122ffa03c28:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:1d11aca37703196b --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3db18122ffa03c28:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:e09915d3625c445e --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3db18122ffa03c28:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:8a50ca9273023412 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3db18122ffa03c28:2:1">飞鹰改</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3db18122ffa03c28:2:2">龙骧</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3db18122ffa03c28:2:3">独角兽</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3db18122ffa03c28:2:4">诺福克</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3db18122ffa03c28:2:5">黑暗界</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:3db18122ffa03c28:2:6">隼鹰</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:436:src-7d080e8d651b:table:3db18122ffa03c28:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
传统轻母队，索敌低一些，没有双海信物可用。
<!-- source-body:437:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:4ce203d9bd9e958b:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:438:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:1e475f0e86a5dce6:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:439:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:e535b5092d48fe82:paragraph -->

#### ②衣笠旗
<!-- source-body:440:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:bd9d644e385ee644:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
标准减伤旗舰。可选带中减的巡洋替换，索敌压力大。
<!-- source-body:441:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:261e235fd10d522b:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:398e1ec222cafac8" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:398e1ec222cafac8" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:398e1ec222cafac8:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:471b1b8f0b08de6d --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:398e1ec222cafac8:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:1b22a2901be73c20 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:398e1ec222cafac8:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:b03d24560d8c0f42 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:398e1ec222cafac8:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:08933e4b449c5153 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:398e1ec222cafac8:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:750cb08ebfc4f892 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:398e1ec222cafac8:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:b8fc0c58aef56544 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:398e1ec222cafac8:2:1">飞鹰改</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:398e1ec222cafac8:2:2">隼鹰</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:398e1ec222cafac8:2:3">昆西</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:398e1ec222cafac8:2:4">衣笠</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:398e1ec222cafac8:2:5">黑暗界</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:398e1ec222cafac8:2:6">恐怖</td></tr></tbody></table></div>

### 3.紫大船
<!-- source-body:449:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:38468ee4ee6c85e9:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
由于缺少对空减伤手段和aoe能力，紫大是航母的天下，配队航系优先。没有很明显的克制关系。
<!-- source-body:450:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:ca75065cf9a15a8e:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
加入航战极限的纯航复制人索敌线在1952。这个条件下，最少需要混编4航（战）+2战列才能满足。当前环境下混编3+3即便有大量索敌SBD飞机和天河也容易T劣。
<!-- source-body:451:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:74458416dd522451:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
默认阵型推荐轮型|复纵阵。
<!-- source-body:452:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:62613f8274eb2991:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:453:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f3b668b2e3019b3e:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:non-prose -->
推荐舰灵：
<!-- source-body:454:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:c8eea752718eba84:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
航战，皇家方舟，鹰，约克城，荷马（甘古特），列萨复制人，大黄蜂。齐柏林复制人一般不考虑。
<!-- source-body:455:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:f277d33ef10487d8:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
混编带罗德尼，威尔士，陆奥等有大减的紫战列。
<!-- source-body:456:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:b2cd60cc178ed83f:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:457:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:b0e057a40f3fd8f6:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
参考阵容
<!-- source-body:458:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:b9d7ac11c4cea818:paragraph -->

#### ①纯航
<!-- source-body:459:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:67a4ec6fbffba8ac:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
在航战加入后有了前排更上一层楼。阵型推荐复纵/轮型
<!-- source-body:460:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:faafba649835e1b6:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:64d63be6db5e0512" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:64d63be6db5e0512" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:64d63be6db5e0512:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:1e33921ceb8cbc71 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:64d63be6db5e0512:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:e07d8096c13b0119 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:64d63be6db5e0512:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:590481bede8bf007 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:64d63be6db5e0512:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:1cb24a6c8fe7755e --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:64d63be6db5e0512:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d508e024d2fd4159 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:64d63be6db5e0512:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:9e4061d6f3690257 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:64d63be6db5e0512:2:1">鹰</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:64d63be6db5e0512:2:2">日向改</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:64d63be6db5e0512:2:3">伊势改</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:64d63be6db5e0512:2:4">皇家方舟</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:64d63be6db5e0512:2:5">列克星敦</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:64d63be6db5e0512:2:6">萨拉托加</td></tr></tbody></table></div>

<!-- source-body:463:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:8d562798fb81d8ed:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:e64d1ddd29ee953a" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:e64d1ddd29ee953a" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:e64d1ddd29ee953a:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:0d1501f789b74d59 --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:e64d1ddd29ee953a:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:436bc00ad96e7d53 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:e64d1ddd29ee953a:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:f38a1813f758de0c --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:e64d1ddd29ee953a:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:8751bc5e0e748bd7 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:e64d1ddd29ee953a:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:90eadc121c15d23d --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:e64d1ddd29ee953a:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:de1131ffa69af36b --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:e64d1ddd29ee953a:2:1">大黄蜂</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:e64d1ddd29ee953a:2:2">伊势改</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:e64d1ddd29ee953a:2:3">日向改</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:e64d1ddd29ee953a:2:4">约克城</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:e64d1ddd29ee953a:2:5">列克星敦</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:e64d1ddd29ee953a:2:6">萨拉托加</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:464:src-7d080e8d651b:table:e64d1ddd29ee953a:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑全sbd可达最高极限索敌
<!-- source-body:465:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:eae4058011cdecc6:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:43c2ea73ea979402" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:43c2ea73ea979402" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:43c2ea73ea979402:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:0572aa14792800ea --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:43c2ea73ea979402:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:f4ec1b3350807e7c --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:43c2ea73ea979402:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:1d99ae7f3553a853 --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:43c2ea73ea979402:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:80a1c77879312ad8 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:43c2ea73ea979402:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:1c5927732eff2c14 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:43c2ea73ea979402:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d6ea841b1f392f59 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:43c2ea73ea979402:2:1">大黄蜂</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:43c2ea73ea979402:2:2">伊势改</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:43c2ea73ea979402:2:3">日向改</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:43c2ea73ea979402:2:4">约克城</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:43c2ea73ea979402:2:5">鹰</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:43c2ea73ea979402:2:6">皇家方舟</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:466:src-7d080e8d651b:table:43c2ea73ea979402:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑无复制人。索敌略低，尽量多索敌sbd、天河打T优
<!-- source-body:467:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:4cbd1b492547cf49:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:468:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:5d36334d93aea9e3:paragraph -->

#### ②荷马旗
<!-- source-body:469:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:a8575d1cb43f0c44:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
减伤旗舰，比较百搭。不刚需复制人。示例：
<!-- source-body:470:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:ff1fc82ffdf215eb:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bd5ff145b58ccf72" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bd5ff145b58ccf72" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bd5ff145b58ccf72:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:6a299eefbc4e3e9c --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bd5ff145b58ccf72:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:5d6beeb771855dd9 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bd5ff145b58ccf72:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:0170b36e38112dda --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bd5ff145b58ccf72:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:1fd8f32a474b6559 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bd5ff145b58ccf72:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:e6f64b36908815b5 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bd5ff145b58ccf72:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:85c0901d59f99461 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bd5ff145b58ccf72:2:1">威尔士亲王</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bd5ff145b58ccf72:2:2">约克城</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bd5ff145b58ccf72:2:3">罗德尼</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bd5ff145b58ccf72:2:4">俄克拉荷马</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bd5ff145b58ccf72:2:5">鹰</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:bd5ff145b58ccf72:2:6">皇家方舟</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:471:src-7d080e8d651b:table:bd5ff145b58ccf72:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
↑3+3的场合索敌可能不够，尽量多索敌装备或加入航战/减少战列
<!-- source-body:472:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:7c7261b44d1646cb:paragraph -->

#### ③T劣龟炮
<!-- source-body:479:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:0d74992a76319e09:paragraph -->

<div class="docx-table-scroll" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:598a7041d1d6a92a" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格，可横向滚动"><table class="docx-table" aria-label="DOCX 原稿表格" data-source-table="src-7d080e8d651b:table:598a7041d1d6a92a" data-grid-columns="6"><tbody><tr><th data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:598a7041d1d6a92a:1:1"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:2463194bdd7e0b3f --></th><th data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:598a7041d1d6a92a:1:2"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:9bc2bcfbd3e79607 --></th><th data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:598a7041d1d6a92a:1:3"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d66624237809ea3b --></th><th data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:598a7041d1d6a92a:1:4"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:d385e52c3ffab272 --></th><th data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:598a7041d1d6a92a:1:5"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:153ab0462e657ff8 --></th><th data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:598a7041d1d6a92a:1:6"><!-- docx-cell-media:src-7d080e8d651b:relation:09d21976fddb2620 --></th></tr><tr><td data-grid-column="1" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:598a7041d1d6a92a:2:1">陆奥</td><td data-grid-column="2" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:598a7041d1d6a92a:2:2">罗德尼</td><td data-grid-column="3" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:598a7041d1d6a92a:2:3">威尔士亲王</td><td data-grid-column="4" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:598a7041d1d6a92a:2:4">纳尔逊</td><td data-grid-column="5" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:598a7041d1d6a92a:2:5">约克城</td><td data-grid-column="6" data-source-cell="src-7d080e8d651b:table:598a7041d1d6a92a:2:6">俄克拉荷马</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<!-- source-body:480:src-7d080e8d651b:table:598a7041d1d6a92a:table -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
相比于金大，紫大没有aoe能力，因此通常是按顺序从前排打到后排。由此出现带大量普列塞的T劣炮队（最少6个）。阵型单纵阵
<!-- source-body:481:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:96567b7c602d236b:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
优点：打航队大优势
<!-- source-body:482:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:b08455144fb08705:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
缺点：T劣，打普通的混编基本稳输
<!-- source-body:483:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:03fbd4dd151752b2:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:484:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:d5eccb898fd0422d:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
特别备注
<!-- source-body:485:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:0edd4d6acf76aa94:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
紫场均推荐优先索敌天赋。
<!-- source-body:486:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:b65be9a5d60f7367:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:487:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:6921af33f67d2ee1:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
甘古特默认和荷马同定位替代，具体效果略弱。
<!-- source-body:488:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:0d02e5429ac9019e:paragraph -->

## （三）海岸警备（蓝场）
<!-- source-body:489:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:ed9e50457dfe0ede:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
蓝场舰灵选择面很窄，基本都是固定一两个阵容，不做过多赘述。因多数舰灵都会改造，导致蓝场几乎是复制人的天下，在玩家没有资源充足到练复制人的情况下不要碰蓝场。
<!-- source-body:490:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:297218e1aa439d28:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:491:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:3172afde094755e6:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
参考阵容
<!-- source-body:492:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:a170cf39aa740ea1:paragraph -->

### 1.蓝小场
<!-- source-body:493:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:1cbc931abc02bb80:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
阵型推荐单纵|复纵阵

### 3.蓝大场
<!-- source-body:513:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:212a17bb57fd84c5:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
推荐伊势日向改造去紫金场。
<!-- source-body:514:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:3be6e328fc19e72c:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
此外蓝大缺少航母，命中防空相对减需，蓝大战列的副炮可用轻巡炮。阵型单纵阵

# 四、结语
<!-- source-body:523:src-7d080e8d651b:heading:99525298de5c2d64:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
再怎么科研也敌不过随机数的大手，从数值上来说许多科研只能算蚊子腿，但同等程度阵容和装备的优化，长期看是有所体现的。
<!-- source-body:524:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:63d88cfa42f33d69:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
本意不是希望大家卷，而是希望开发更多新阵容。科研会让镜像阵容打内战有优势，变阵也能让环境百花齐放。有更多机会让自己的XP发灵光发热，不必自限于一些条条框框。
<!-- source-body:525:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:e3365d24008c1656:paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:526:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:85b93d7b4174abc8:paragraph -->

<!-- article-paragraph:prose -->
所以大家一起来愉快肘击吧！
<!-- source-body:527:src-7d080e8d651b:paragraph:149f189752efe083:paragraph -->
